# terok — Functional Specification

What the system does, stated as observable behaviour. Derived independently from the
upstream program's interface definitions, handler implementations and documentation;
its test suite is not a warrant here. Equivalence is functional — identical file
layout, internal names and byte-exact output are explicitly out of scope.

Every row carries an executable check and the surface it was derived from. `requires`
names the capability the check needs on the test host: real `podman` and real `nft`,
never a fake.

**555 behaviours.**

| slice                                                                           | behaviours |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------- |
| [Task lifecycle — `task`, `login`](#task-lifecycle)                             | 85         |
| [Projects and agents — `project`, `agents`](#project-agents)                    | 72         |
| [Images and installation — `image`, `setup`, `uninstall`](#image-setup)         | 54         |
| [Credentials — `auth`, `vault`, `clearance`, `ssh`](#credentials)               | 54         |
| [Egress enforcement — `shield`, `gate`, `dbus`](#egress-shield)                 | 73         |
| [Sandbox and execution — `sandbox`, `executor`](#sandbox-executor)              | 89         |
| [Operations — `sickbay`, `panic`, `config`, `acp`, `completions`](#ops-surface) | 62         |

| TASK-LIFECYCLE-029 | A task ID is a 5-char Crockford-base32 string, lowercase: a 16-letter head char (one of `ghjkmnpqrstvwxyz`, i.e. `g-z` minus `i l o u`), then a decimal digit, then 3 more Crockford chars (`0-9 a-z` minus `i l o u`). Shape regex: `[ghjkmnp-tv-z][0-9][0-9a-hjkmnp-tv-z]{3}`. | PROJECT-AGENTS-12 | The selection grammar accepted by `agents set` (and `project agents set`, `project build --agents`, the wizard) is: the literal `all`; a comma list of names; a name prefixed `-` to exclude; `all,-name` combines both. Matching is case-insensitive and whitespace-tolerant; an all-excludes input (e.g. `-vibe`) seeds from every installable entry.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | wheel roster/loader.py:395-433,502-518                                                                                                                                                    | `TEROK_CONFIG_FILE=/tmp/t/config.yml terok agents set "ALL,-VIBE"` → exit 0, value written is `ALL,-VIBE` (verbatim); `terok agents set "-vibe"` → exit 0.                                                                                                                                 | none     |
| PROJECT-AGENTS-13 | Selection dependencies are transitive: picking a provider that declares `depends_on` pulls its dependency in (e.g. `blablador` also installs `opencode`); an exclude that names a dependency of a kept entry silently drops it. ## egress-shield

### Egress enforcement — `shield`, `gate`, `dbus`

## sandbox-executor

### Sandbox and execution — `sandbox`, `executor`

| id                  | behaviour                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | derived from                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | executable check                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | requires             |
| ------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------- |
| SANDBOX-EXECUTOR-01 | `terok` exposes the full executor tree as `terok executor <verb>` and the full sandbox tree as `terok sandbox <verb>` (and as shortcuts `terok vault …`, `terok gate …`, `terok ssh …`, `terok dbus …`). Shortcut and deep path resolve to the SAME command object, so a wrap applied at one entry applies at the other.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | T:cli/main.py:36,55-70,362-370; E:_tree.py:44-96; docs/usage.md:54-92                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | `terok --help` lists `executor`, `sandbox`, `vault`, `gate`, `ssh`, `dbus` roots; `terok executor run --help` prints the executor `run` help; `terok vault --help` and `terok sandbox vault --help` show the same vault subtree                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | dbus                 |
| SANDBOX-EXECUTOR-02 | Per-container executor verbs accept two identity forms: a raw container id/name, or `<project>/<task>`, resolved via terok's task store. Same dual form as `git push origin master` vs `origin/master`; `terok task <verb> p t` and `terok task <verb> p/t` are interchangeable for the same reason.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | docs/usage.md:71-81; T:cli/main.py:55-70                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | `terok executor stop myproj/abc12` (container exists) stops the task container; `terok executor stop myproj-cli-abc12` stops the same container by raw name                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | podman               |
| SANDBOX-EXECUTOR-03 | `terok-executor` top-level options must precede the subcommand (`terok-executor --config /path run claude .`). Options: `-h/--help`, `--version` (prints `terok-executor <version>`), `--config PATH` (sets `TEROK_CONFIG_FILE` for the invocation, bypassing layered system/user lookup), `--raw` (equivalent to `--config /dev/null` — sandbox/executor dataclass defaults only). No verb → help on stdout, exit 1. Unknown verb → argparse error, exit 2. `[LIVE]`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | E:cli.py:60-125; E:_tree.py:44-96                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `/tmp/terok-venv/bin/terok-executor --version` → `terok-executor 0.4.0a19`, exit 0; `terok-executor --raw show-config` prints defaults with no config.yml read; `terok-executor bogus` → usage error, exit 2                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | none                 |

| TUI-06 | `P` is an arm-then-fire panic: the first press turns the button red with `PRESS AGAIN TO PANIC` and starts a 5-second auto-disarm timer; a second press within 5 s fires the emergency sequence (shields up, proxy and git-gate stopped) and toasts a report (`Containers found: N / Shields raised: N / Supervisors killed: N / Vault: passphrase destroyed (re-supply to unlock)`); the button then returns to `PANIC`; a single press with no second press auto-disarms after 5 s                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | src/terok/tui/widgets/panic_button.py:24-91, app.py:1956-1998, task_actions.py (panic worker); live capture                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | In the TUI press `P` → button reads `PRESS AGAIN TO PANIC`; press `P` again → toast `Containers found: 0 ...` and button reads `PANIC`; wait 6 s after arming → button auto-returns to `PANIC`                                                                                      | tty          |
